SOURCE INVENTORY

GENERATED SOURCE METADATA

Source API lifecycle

Introduction, deprecation, and removal history for public source declarations.

Generated from public Aevix source declarations. Presence is not execution evidence. A value of none means no deprecation or removal version is scheduled; it does not promise permanent compatibility. @version remains documentation version metadata.

Compiler-injected standard-library declarations are included with their virtual module names. Their placeholder bodies and signatures describe compiler binding surfaces rather than ordinary Aevix implementations. Callable rows marked Compiler binding declaration deliberately omit those placeholder signatures: a provider declaration such as void new is not the typed return contract. The raw declaration is retained only as provider_declaration in metadata. This table does not claim complete CPU catalog support.

Current scoped support includes exact floating representation, explicit-endian byte codecs and bit helpers, bounded overlap copying, shared Ordering, Range inspection and owned Task/TaskScope execution. The library foundations explain their ownership, execution and version limits. Declaration presence alone does not extend those limits.

Verified development checkpoint

Recorded snapshot: 2026-10-18-39088693. Local CTest passed 138/138 in 703.69 seconds on macOS ARM64. The recorded inventory contains 3797 public declarations with current lifecycle metadata.

Compiler binary SHA256: 390886932eb9e335dcc8094cdb62fd397f36ae1ed4b8387eb99ac95145930d93.

This result belongs only to the recorded binary and inventory snapshot. Selected gates retain the scope recorded in the checkpoint metadata; later source declarations and compiler changes are unverified by it. Passing the existing tests does not complete the CPU library catalog or verify every target.
